It’s so clear that I’ll answer them out loud, and then I realize no one else heard anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like people are plotting against you or that you’re being targeted in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d call it plotting. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. And I don’t feel like my thoughts are being controlled. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. It’s more just… I get uneasy sometimes, but not like messages are being sent to me or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were being controlled by some outside force or influence?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. I haven’t really noticed gestures or situations like that, no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain actions, words, or events happening around you were specifically about you, even if others didn’t seem to react to them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I can feel self-conscious sometimes, but I haven’t noticed actions or words around me feeling like they were specifically about me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things people say or do, even if they don’t seem related, are actually about you or meant for you to notice?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Sometimes I might wonder if someone’s being a little rude or if I’m being noticed, but I haven’t really felt like unrelated things people say or do are secretly meant for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people say or do, even when they don’t seem connected to you, are actually directed at you or have a hidden meaning for you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I’ve answered that a few ways, but no, I haven’t really noticed things having a hidden meaning for me or being directed at me like that.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. Can you tell me what's been bothering you or what brings you in today?\n\nPatient: I came in because I keep hearing people talking about me. It happens through the conference room walls, or sometimes from my phone even when it’s turned off. It sounds like board members or staff saying I’m ruining the company. It’s so clear that I’ll answer them out loud, and then I realize no one else heard anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like people are plotting against you or that you’re being targeted in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d call it plotting. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. And I don’t feel like my thoughts are being controlled. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. It’s more just… I get uneasy sometimes, but not like messages are being sent to me or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were being controlled by some outside force or influence?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. I haven’t really noticed gestures or situations like that, no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain actions, words, or events happening around you were specifically about you, even if others didn’t seem to react to them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I can feel self-conscious sometimes, but I haven’t noticed actions or words around me feeling like they were specifically about me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things people say or do, even if they don’t seem related, are actually about you or meant for you to notice?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Sometimes I might wonder if someone’s being a little rude or if I’m being noticed, but I haven’t really felt like unrelated things people say or do are secretly meant for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people say or do, even when they don’t seem connected to you, are actually directed at you or have a hidden meaning for you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I’ve answered that a few ways, but no, I haven’t really noticed things having a hidden meaning for me or being directed at me like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were influenced by something outside of yourself, like someone or something was making you think or do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think I’ve felt that. I mean, I can feel pressured by people or situations, but not like something outside me is controlling my thoughts or making me do things.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts or actions were being influenced or controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone or something else was inserting thoughts into your mind or making you do things against your will?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had thoughts put into my mind or felt like someone was making me do things against my will. Sometimes I feel stressed or influenced by what’s going on around me, but not controlled like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something to be true that others insist isn’t real or accurate, even though you’re completely sure it is?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I’ve been sure about things before and had people disagree with me, but not in the sense that everyone’s telling me it isn’t real and I’m holding onto it anyway.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. I mean, I can feel self-conscious sometimes, but I haven’t noticed actions or words around me feeling like they were specifically about me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things people say or do, even if they don’t seem related, are actually about you or meant for you to notice?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Sometimes I might wonder if someone’s being a little rude or if I’m being noticed, but I haven’t really felt like unrelated things people say or do are secretly meant for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people say or do, even when they don’t seem connected to you, are actually directed at you or have a hidden meaning for you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I’ve answered that a few ways, but no, I haven’t really noticed things having a hidden meaning for me or being directed at me like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were influenced by something outside of yourself, like someone or something was making you think or do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think I’ve felt that. I mean, I can feel pressured by people or situations, but not like something outside me is controlling my thoughts or making me do things.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts or actions were being influenced or controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone or something else was inserting thoughts into your mind or making you do things against your will?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had thoughts put into my mind or felt like someone was making me do things against my will. Sometimes I feel stressed or influenced by what’s going on around me, but not controlled like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something to be true that others insist isn’t real or accurate, even though you’re completely sure it is?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I’ve been sure about things before and had people disagree with me, but not in the sense that everyone’s telling me it isn’t real and I’m holding onto it anyway.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain events, comments, or actions by others had a direct, personal connection to you, even if others didn’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I might take a comment personally sometimes, like anyone would, but I don’t feel like random events or other people’s actions are specifically meant for me when others don’t see it that way.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. It’s so clear that I’ll answer them out loud, and then I realize no one else heard anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like people are plotting against you or that you’re being targeted in some way?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d call it plotting. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. And I don’t feel like my thoughts are being controlled. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. It’s more just… I get uneasy sometimes, but not like messages are being sent to me or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were being controlled by some outside force or influence?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. I haven’t really noticed gestures or situations like that, no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain actions, words, or events happening around you were specifically about you, even if others didn’t seem to react to them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I can feel self-conscious sometimes, but I haven’t noticed actions or words around me feeling like they were specifically about me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things people say or do, even if they don’t seem related, are actually about you or meant for you to notice?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Sometimes I might wonder if someone’s being a little rude or if I’m being noticed, but I haven’t really felt like unrelated things people say or do are secretly meant for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people say or do, even when they don’t seem connected to you, are actually directed at you or have a hidden meaning for you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I’ve answered that a few ways, but no, I haven’t really noticed things having a hidden meaning for me or being directed at me like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were influenced by something outside of yourself, like someone or something was making you think or do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think I’ve felt that. I mean, I can feel pressured by people or situations, but not like something outside me is controlling my thoughts or making me do things.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts or actions were being influenced or controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone or something else was inserting thoughts into your mind or making you do things against your will?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had thoughts put into my mind or felt like someone was making me do things against my will. Sometimes I feel stressed or influenced by what’s going on around me, but not controlled like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something to be true that others insist isn’t real or accurate, even though you’re completely sure it is?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I’ve been sure about things before and had people disagree with me, but not in the sense that everyone’s telling me it isn’t real and I’m holding onto it anyway.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain events, comments, or actions by others had a direct, personal connection to you, even if others didn’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I might take a comment personally sometimes, like anyone would, but I don’t feel like random events or other people’s actions are specifically meant for me when others don’t see it that way.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something strange was happening inside your body or mind, like your thoughts were being taken away or that you were being made to do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed anything like that. I don’t feel like my thoughts are being taken away, and I don’t feel like something is making me do things without my control.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever held onto a belief that something was true, even when others gave you strong evidence or proof that it wasn’t?\n\nPatient: No, I can’t think of a time like that. If someone showed me clear proof I was wrong, I might not like it, but I’d usually accept it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people do or say, even if they seem unrelated, are secretly about you or meant to send you a message?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. And I don’t feel like my thoughts are being controlled. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. It’s more just… I get uneasy sometimes, but not like messages are being sent to me or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were being controlled by some outside force or influence?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. I haven’t really noticed gestures or situations like that, no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain actions, words, or events happening around you were specifically about you, even if others didn’t seem to react to them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I can feel self-conscious sometimes, but I haven’t noticed actions or words around me feeling like they were specifically about me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things people say or do, even if they don’t seem related, are actually about you or meant for you to notice?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Sometimes I might wonder if someone’s being a little rude or if I’m being noticed, but I haven’t really felt like unrelated things people say or do are secretly meant for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people say or do, even when they don’t seem connected to you, are actually directed at you or have a hidden meaning for you?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I’ve answered that a few ways, but no, I haven’t really noticed things having a hidden meaning for me or being directed at me like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions or thoughts were influenced by something outside of yourself, like someone or something was making you think or do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think I’ve felt that. I mean, I can feel pressured by people or situations, but not like something outside me is controlling my thoughts or making me do things.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts or actions were being influenced or controlled by something outside of yourself, like someone or something else was inserting thoughts into your mind or making you do things against your will?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t had thoughts put into my mind or felt like someone was making me do things against my will. Sometimes I feel stressed or influenced by what’s going on around me, but not controlled like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something to be true that others insist isn’t real or accurate, even though you’re completely sure it is?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I’ve been sure about things before and had people disagree with me, but not in the sense that everyone’s telling me it isn’t real and I’m holding onto it anyway.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain events, comments, or actions by others had a direct, personal connection to you, even if others didn’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I might take a comment personally sometimes, like anyone would, but I don’t feel like random events or other people’s actions are specifically meant for me when others don’t see it that way.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something strange was happening inside your body or mind, like your thoughts were being taken away or that you were being made to do things without your control?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t noticed anything like that. I don’t feel like my thoughts are being taken away, and I don’t feel like something is making me do things without my control.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever held onto a belief that something was true, even when others gave you strong evidence or proof that it wasn’t?\n\nPatient: No, I can’t think of a time like that. If someone showed me clear proof I was wrong, I might not like it, but I’d usually accept it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people do or say, even if they seem unrelated, are secretly about you or meant to send you a message?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I mean, sometimes people say things and I wonder what they meant, but I don’t feel like unrelated things are secretly messages for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things happening around you—like things people say, gestures, or events—have a direct connection to you, even if they don’t seem related to others?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I’ve noticed that. People say things or things happen, but I don’t usually take it as being directly connected to me if there’s no clear reason.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

I don’t feel like my thoughts are being taken away, and I don’t feel like something is making me do things without my control.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever held onto a belief that something was true, even when others gave you strong evidence or proof that it wasn’t?\n\nPatient: No, I can’t think of a time like that. If someone showed me clear proof I was wrong, I might not like it, but I’d usually accept it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things people do or say, even if they seem unrelated, are secretly about you or meant to send you a message?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I just feel like people are talking about me or watching what I’m doing sometimes, but I’m not sure if they’re actually targeting me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing these voices, and have they been causing you any distress or affecting your daily life?\n\nPatient: It’s been about three months now, I think. The voices from the walls or from my phone started around then, and yes… it’s been really upsetting and hard to just get through the day normally.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that someone was trying to harm you, control your thoughts, or that events were being directed against you in a hidden or unusual way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone is trying to harm me, no. It’s more like… I get this feeling people are paying attention to me or talking about me, but I’m not sure beyond that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you, like things on TV or random events, were specifically directed at you or had special meaning for you?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t really noticed TV or random things feeling like they’re meant just for me. I haven’t felt like something outside of me is controlling what I do or what I think. I mean, I get uneasy sometimes, but not like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain situations, gestures, or comments were directed at you in a personal way, even if others didn’t seem to notice it?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean, sometimes I feel like people are noticing me, or I get uncomfortable with a comment someone makes, but I can’t say it feels definitely directed at me in some special way. People say things or things happen, but I don’t usually take it as being directly connected to me if there’s no clear reason.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period where you felt down, hopeless, or lost interest in things for most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks?\n\nPatient: Yes. It’s been more than two weeks, definitely. I used to be the one pushing every idea at work, calling people after openings, keeping everything moving. Now I can sit at my desk for hours just staring at the agenda. I barely talk to my husband or my staff, and the strange part is I don’t feel much about it. Even the work I used to really love just feels like… nothing.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost interest or pleasure in activities you used to enjoy, like hobbies, work, or spending time with loved ones?\n\nPatient: Yes.
